def project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = v.size
    if n == 1: return np.maximum(v, S)?? Actually if v[0]<S, add S-v.

   # Riemannian projected subgradient: d = -g + cmean? Since sum constraint.

   if any negative: a_new = project_simplex(a_new, S)
   else normalize to S (a_new /= a_new.sum()*S)
   # step adapt: every few, compare cmax
```

   - n=S? Actually sequence length n; target sum S=sum_target.
   - Initialize normalized.
   - best.
   - while time:
       elapsed fraction f; tau=tau_hi*(tau_lo/tau_hi)**f.
       c=conv(a)
       # if cmax update best
       cm=c.max(); shift c-cm; q=exp(c_shift/tau); q/=sum(q)
       g=2*corr(a,q)
       # step size adapt: lr = lr0 * (some norm?)

```
But if g has high values (n*?), step too large. Could set step = lr / max(gmax/S, 1e-6). If we want move O(S/n)? For random gradient, optimal step ~? Let's empirically choose based on score change. Since no run, need safe.

Alternative multiplicative update avoids step size sensitivity:
```
def run_mir(a0, temp, rate, budget):
  while time:
    c=conva
    q=softmax(c/temp) # sum 1
    G=corr(a,q) # gradient log
    # smooth G: if all zero? 
    # a *= exp(-rate*G/G.mean())
    exponent = -rate * (G - G.mean()) / max(np.std(G), eps)
    # clip exponent to [-clip, clip]
    a = a * np.exp(np.clip(exponent, -clip, clip))
    a = normalize(a)
```
This is stable. We can combine rate annealing. But mirror descent may move slowly.

Could use "exponentiated gradient on active top" with rate ~1.

while time:

  cmax update

Let's outline final code functions:

```
def c_and_score(a):
   c = conv(a)
   return c, float(c.max())
```

`conv(a)`: use N from `fft_n(a.size)`, `rfft`, `irfft`. Because a normalized sum S. Ensure c nonnegative small negatives set 0? Convolution of nonnegative should nonnegative; FFT roundoff. `c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)`? This may slightly affect objective/gradient but okay. For score, use max(raw) maybe roundoff; set tiny negative to 0. If roundoff positive tiny no issue.

`correlate_valid(a, w, N)`: as above. Need use same N.

`project_simplex` robust.

`score_sequence(a)`: normalize? We'll maintain normalized. But final evaluate might scale. Return evaluate_sequence(a.tolist()) best.

Optimization 1: `optimize_softmax(a0, tau_hi, tau_lo, alpha, budget, verbose)`.
Pseudo:
```
n=len(a); S=math.sqrt(2*n); N=next_power2(2*n-1)
a=normalize(a0,S); best=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a); c=conv(a)
step = alpha/n? maybe 10/n
momentum=0.0? v=None
t_accept=0
while time < budget:
   f = elapsed/budget
   tau = tau_hi*(tau_lo/tau_hi)**f
   if c is None or step%? recompute c? We have c.
   sc_current = c.max()
   if sc_current < best_sc: update best
   cm = c.max()
   # softmax stable
   z = (c - cm)/tau
   # avoid underflow? exp okay if z min large negative; use np.exp
   q = np.exp(z)
   qs = q.sum()
   if qs == 0: q = np.full(len(c),1/len(c))
   else q/=qs
   g = 2*correlate_valid(a,q,N)
   # tangent: subtract mean (maybe weighted by positive?)
   g = g - np.mean(g)
   # normalize
   gnorm = np.max(np.abs(g))
   if gnorm > 0: g = g/gnorm
   if v is None: v = g
   else: v = momentum*v + (1-momentum)*g
   v = v - np.mean(v)
   # choose step: base = step * S/n? Wait g normalized max1, if we multiply by step_param.
   a_new = a - current_step * g
   a_new = project_simplex(a_new,S)
   # evaluate candidate score by conv? expensive. Do every eval_every or adaptive.
```

Implement `fft_conv(a, N=None)`:
```
n = a.size
if N is None: N=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, N)
return c[:2*n-1].copy()
```

Implement `fft_corr(a, w, N)`:
```
A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
x = np.fft.irfft(A * np.conj(W), N)
return np.concatenate((x[:1], x[N - a.size + 1:]))
```
Potential indexing if N - n + 1 == 0? N≥2n-1, for n=1 N=1? N - n +1=1, concat x[:1]+x[1:] empty -> ok. For n=3000, N=8192, start=5193. length=3000? 8192-5193=2999 plus 1 =3000. Good.

But let's double-check correlation formula with a small numerical example mentally using DFT properties. DFT of padded a: A_k=∑_t a_t e^{-2πi kt/N}. W_k=∑_s w_s e^{-...}. If `np.irfft(A * np.conj(W), N)`, output y_m = 1/N ∑ A_k conj(W_k) e^{2πi km/N}. Conj(W_k)=∑_s w_s e^{2πi ks/N}. Product =∑_{t,s} a_t w_s e^{-2πi k(t-s)/N}. Inverse gives ∑_{t,s} a_t w_s δ_{m, t-s mod N} =∑_t a_t w_{t-m mod N}. So y_m=∑_t a_t w_{(t-m) mod N}. We want h_i=∑_j a_j w_{i+j} for i=0..n-1. For i=0: m=0 ok. For i>0: set m=N-i: t-m mod N = t+i (for t < n, t+i ≤2n-2 < N). So y_{N-i}=h_i. So h = [y[0], y[N-1], y[N-2], ..., y[N-n+1]]? Wait careful: We want h ordered i=1 -> y[N-1], i=2 -> y[N-2], ..., i=n-1 -> y[N-(n-1)] = y[N-n+1]. So slice y[N-n+1:N-1]? reversed! Our earlier slice `x[N-n+1:]` gives [y[N-n+1], y[N-n+2], ..., y[N-1]], which is [h_{n-1}, h_{n-2},...,h_1], reversed! Let's test n=2: N=4. h0=y0, h1=y3. Slice [N-n+1:N-1]? N-n+1=3, N-1=3 empty? Wait need include y3. If slice `x[N-n+1:]` = x[3:4] = [y3] = h1. Good ordering for n=2 because length1. For n=3,N=6? N≥5, choose 8. h1=y7? N-1; h2=y8-2? N=8: h1=y7, h2=y6. `x[N-n+1:] = x[6:8] = [y6,y7]` = [h2,h1] reversed! Indeed. For n=2 reversed length1 no issue; for n=3 wrong.
So we need `h = np.concatenate((x[:1], x[N-n+1:N][::-1]?))`? Let's derive: We need [x[0], x[N-1], x[N-2], ..., x[N-n+1]]. That's x[0] + reverse(x[N-n+1:N]). So:
```
tail = x[N-n+1:N]  # length n-1, indices from N-n+1 to N-1
h = np.concatenate((x[:1], tail[::-1]))
```
Test n=3: tail [x6,x7], reverse [x7,x6] = h1,h2. Good.
Let's ensure for N=2n-1 exactly? n=3,N=5: tail x[3:5]=[x3,x4], reverse [x4,x3]. h1=y4 (N-1), h2=y3 (N-2), correct. Good.
So implement reversed.

Alternative could use `np.roll`? We'll do reverse.

Let's verify FFT convolution order c: product A*A gives cyclic convolution y_m=∑ a_t a_s δ_{m,t+s}. For m=0..2n-2, correct. No reversal. Good.

Now project_simplex standard. Need maybe use `np.sort(v)` ascending. Standard code:
```
def project_simplex(v, s):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([max(s, 0.0)])  # projection: if v[0] maybe less, must s; if >s, s.
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * (np.arange(1, n+1) > (cssv - s)))[0][-1]
    theta = (cssv[rho] - s) / (rho + 1)
    w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    # correct numeric
    diff = s - w.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        idx = w.argmax()
        w[idx] += diff
        if w[idx] < 0: # rare
            w = np.maximum(w,0); w += (s-w.sum())/n
    return w
```
If all v are very large? Works. If v has NaN? ensure not.

`normalize_sum(a, S)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float)
a=np.maximum(a,0)
s=a.sum()
if s <= 0: return np.full_like(a, S/a.size)
return a * (S/s)
```

Let's define `run_softmax_pgd`:
```
def run_softmax_pgd(a0, tau_hi, tau_lo, alpha, momentum, budget, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_sum(a0)
    n=a.size; S=math.sqrt(2*n); N=next_power2(2*n-1)
    c = fft_conv(a,N)
    best = a.copy(); best_sc = float(c.max())
    t0 = time.monotonic(); t_end=t0+budget
    step = max(1e-5, alpha * S / n)  # normalized grad? We'll normalize g to max1, so step absolute.
    v = np.zeros_like(a)
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
       f = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-t0)/max(budget,1e-6))
       tau = tau_hi * (tau_lo/tau_hi)**f
       if tau < 1e-6: tau=1e-6
       # maybe update c if no conv? we have c current
       cm = c.max()
       if cm < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best_sc=cm; best=a.copy()
       q = softmax_weights(c, tau)
       g = 2.0 * fft_corr(a, q, N)
       g = g - g.mean()
       gmax = g.max(abs)
       if gmax <= 1e-14: break
       g /= gmax
       if momentum > 0:
           v = momentum * v + (1-momentum)*g
           v = v - v.mean()
       else:
           v = g
       a_new = project_simplex(a - step * v, S)
       c_new = fft_conv(a_new, N)
       sc_new = float(c_new.max())
       if sc_new < cm - 1e-12:  # current cm = c.max()
           a = a_new; c = c_new; cm = sc_new
           if step < 10.0 * S / n: step *= 1.02
       else:
           # maybe keep a, adjust c? c unchanged; velocity? 
           step *= 0.8
           # if too small, reset v? 
           if step < 1e-8: step = 1e-8
       it +=1
       if verbose and it % 1000 ==0: print(...)
    # final best
    if best_sc > objective_norm(a?): best maybe.
    return best, best_sc
```

Harmonic `run_harmonic(a0, budget, tau, alpha)`:
```
a=normalize; S; N; c=conv; best...
while time:
   cm=c.max(); update best
   q=softmax(c/tau)  # positive
   G=fft_corr(a,q,N)  # no 2
   G=np.maximum(G, 1e-15)
   # log update
   logG = np.log(G); logG -= logG.mean()
   # Maybe exponent alpha*h? Use a *= exp(-alpha*logG)
   exp_arg = -alpha*logG
   exp_arg = np.clip(exp_arg, -3, 3)

Viol+harmonic: We can incorporate target in `run_harmonic` by weights q = mix softmax all and violation. But simple.

Maybe the harmonic update is the key. Let's analyze stationarity: For active q, G_i = corr(q,a). The condition for Lagrange optimum of minimizing ∑q_k c_k with fixed sum is G_i constant. The harmonic update `a_i *= 1/G_i` aims to make `a_i G_i` constant, not G_i constant. If instead we use multiplicative Euler on G: `a_i *= exp(-η G_i)`, then at fixed point `G_i` constant? For gradient of log objective? Let's derive. We want minimize F=∑q c. Gradient =2G. Multiplicative gradient descent in log variables: `a_i ← a_i exp(-η ∂F/∂a_i) = a_i exp(-2η G_i)`. If fixed point ignoring normalization, G_i constant (because any coordinate with G_i above mean reduces). So log update `exp(-η G_i)` is appropriate, not log(G). The previous `1/G` is Newton-like maybe but stationary at a_i/G_i constant, different. Let's use `exp(-η G_i)` for gradient. Normalize G (e.g., subtract mean) to avoid scaling: `a *= exp(-η (G - G.mean())/scale)`. This is multiplicative PGD. It may be more stable and has correct stationary condition. Good.

So `run_harmonic` maybe rename `run_multiplicative` with modes sm/hard/viol:
- q weights; G=corr(a,q)
- `r = G - G.mean()` (or /scale)
- `a *= exp(-eta * r)`; normalize.
This is equivalent to projected gradient in log coordinates. Step eta relative. Use eta small 0.1-1. This may be more robust than Euclidean project and preserves positivity. It also automatically keeps sum after normalize. It may be faster than projection (no sort). Let's implement this as primary.

`run_multiplicative(a0, budget, mode='sm', tau_hi, tau_lo, eta0, eta_min, verbose)`:
```
eta = eta0
while time:
   c=conv
   sc update
   q = weights(c)
   G = fft_corr(a,q,N)
   # scale r
   r = G - G.mean()
   # If r all zero, break
   rmax = np.max(np.abs(r))
   if rmax ==0: break
   # choose scale = rmax? Then exponent max eta*? If r normalized to max1, exponent max=eta.
   r = r / rmax
   # maybe use eta anneal
   a = a * np.exp(-eta * r)
   a = normalize_sum(a,S)
   # recompute c next loop
```
If r normalized to max1, eta can be 0.1-1.0. This reduces coordinates with high active product. It is like mirror descent. Since objective homogeneous, normalize. This may converge and avoid nonnegative projection.

Can include momentum? In multiplicative, `log a` update; velocity on r: `v = μ v + (1-μ)r`. Use.

This is simpler and likely stable. Let's test concept: If q weight on max bin, G_i high for positions contributing to max. Reducing them lowers bin; normalization increases others, possibly reducing sum? It is exactly gradient in log-domain with adaptive relative step.

We can use this with different q:
- softmax tau anneal (global smoothing): weight all bins with high emphasis.
- hard active: q indicator near max; r may be sparsy; normalize r by max so exponent eta. May jump cycles; use small eta (0.1) and maybe smooth q by adding 0.05*softmax to all to keep G positive.
- violation target: q = max(c-target,0). If no violation, q small; use softmax.

This algorithm may be enough. Combine with Euclidean PGD? The multiplicative may have its own local minima but different; can alternate.

Let's implement `run_mirror` with mode and adaptive eta:
- Track score; if score improves increase eta up to eta_max; if worsens reduce. Similar to gradient.
- Use `prev_sc` and compare after update.
Pseudo:
```
eta = eta0; prev_sc=inf
while:
   c=conv; sc=c.max(); if sc<best update
   q=make_weights
   G=fft_corr
   r=G-G.mean(); rmax=...
   if rmax<eps: break
   r/=rmax
   # maybe smoothing: r = tanh(r) to limit outliers
   a = a * exp(-eta*r)
   a=normalize
   c_new=conv; sc_new=max
   if sc_new < sc: eta = min(eta_max, eta*1.05)
   else: eta = max(eta_min, eta*0.8)
   c=c_new
```
Again one conv per loop (plus candidate? We compute c at start and after? We can avoid double by computing c at end and then compare? Need q from current c; after update compute new c and compare to old sc. So one conv per iteration plus correlation. Good.
At loop start c from previous end. First compute c for a. Loop:
```
sc=c.max(); best update
q=weights(c)
G=corr
update a
c=conv(a); sc=sc_new

def corr_from_A(a,q,A,N): W=np.fft.rfft(q,N); x=irfft(A*conj(W),N); return concat reverse tail

Weight functions:
```
def make_softmax(c, tau):
    cm=c.max()
    z=(c-cm)/tau
    z=np.clip(z, -50, 0)
    q=np.exp(z)
    s=q.sum()
    if s<=0: q=np.full_like(c, 1/(len(c)))
    else: q/=s
    return q

def make_active(c, eps_frac=0.01, add_soft=0.1):
    cm=c.max()
    thr=cm - eps_frac*cm
    q=(c >= thr).astype(float)
    # add small softmax to all? 
    if add_soft>0:
        s=make_softmax(c, eps_frac*max(cm,1e-6))
        q = (1-add_soft)*q/q.sum() + add_soft*s
    else q/=q.sum()
    return q

def make_violation(c, target, p=2, soft_tau=0.05):
    excess=np.maximum(c-target,0)
    s=excess.sum()
    if s <= 1e-9:
       return make_softmax(c, soft_tau)
    if p ==1: q=excess
    else: q=excess**p
    q /= q.sum()
    return q
```
In `make_active`, if q sum 0? At least max meets.

Potential issue: q sum 1, G scale ~S/L. r normalized max1, eta relative. Good.

def run_euclidean(a0,budget,mode='sm',tau_hi=0.1,tau_lo=0.001,alpha=1.0,momentum=0.5,verbose=False):
   a=positive_protect; S;N; c,A=conv_ret; prev_sc; best
   step=alpha*S/n
   v=zeros
   while time:
      sc=c.max(); update best
      q=weights(c)
      g=2*corr_from_A(a,q,A,N)
      g-=g.mean(); norm
      v=mom*v+(1-mom)*g; v-=mean
      a_new=project_simplex(a-step*v,S)
      a_new=positive_protect? no.
      c,A=conv_ret(a_new)
      if c.max() < sc: step*=1.03; v? 
      else: step*=0.9; v*=0.5
```

Detailed functions:

`next_power2(x)`:
```
def next_power2(x):
    if x <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()
```

`normalize_sum(a,S)`:
```
def normalize_sum(a, S):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    n=a.size
    if s <= 1e-300:
       a = np.full(n, max(S/n, 1e-300))
       return a
    a *= S/s
    return a
```

`positive_floor(a,S,floor=1e-10)`:
```
eps = max(floor, S*1e-12)
a = np.maximum(a, eps)
return normalize_sum(a,S)
```
But if floor too high for n large? S/n ~√(2/n); eps S*1e-12 negligible.

`conv_ret(a,N)`:
```
A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, N)
L = 2*a.size-1
c = c[:L]
c[np.abs(c) < 1e-12] = 0.0? maybe not.
c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)  # remove negative roundoff
return c, A
```
If tiny negative max? max okay. Setting negative to 0 slightly changes sum c? Sum should S^2; not important for weights. However clipping negative could bias? Very small roundoff only.

`corr_from_A(a, q, A, N)`:
```
n=a.size
W=np.fft.rfft(q,N)
x=np.fft.irfft(A*np.conj(W), N)
return np.concatenate((x[:1], x[N-n+1:N][::-1]))
```
If n=1, slice x[1:1] empty. Good.

`objective_from_c(c)` = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else inf.

`make_weights_sm(c, tau)`: as above.

q/=q.sum(); return q
```

Top-level:
```
def smooth_objective(theta, basis, tau, N):
    z = basis @ theta
    z = z - z.max()
    a = np.exp(z)
    c = fft_conv(a, N)
    cm = c.max()
    if cm <=0: return 0
    zc = (c - cm) / tau
    zc = np.clip(zc, -50, 0)
    return cm + tau * float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(zc))))
```

Let's implement `smooth_search` lightweight with random direction, maybe useful. Top-level:
```
def build_basis(n, K=8):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    basis=np.empty((n,K),dtype=np.float64)
    col=np.ones(n)
    for j in range(K): basis[:,j]=col; col*=x
    return basis
```
`smooth_objective(theta, basis, tau, N)`:
```
def smooth_objective(theta, basis, tau, N):
    z = basis @ theta
    z = z - z.max()
    a = np.exp(z)
    c = fft_conv(a,N)
    cm = float(c.max())
    if cm <= 0: return 0.0
    t = (c-cm)/tau
    t = np.clip(t, -50, 0)
    s = float(np.sum(np.exp(t)))
    return cm + tau*math.log(s)
```
`smooth_score_from_theta(theta,basis,N)`:
```
 z=...; a=exp; c=fft_conv; return a,max(c)
```
`smooth_search(n,budget,seed,current_sc)`:
```
if budget < 20: return None, inf
K=8
basis=build_basis(n,K)
N=next_power2(2*n-1)
tau=0.05
rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
best_a=None; best_sc=inf
t0; t_end=t0+budget
start_thetas = [zeros, rng.normal(0,0.7,K), rng.normal(0,1,K)]
for theta0 in starts:
    if time remaining <10: break
    theta=theta0.copy()
    # initial random direction search for 1/3 budget per start? 
    f=smooth_objective(theta,...)
    best_f=f
    step=1.0
    while time < t_end - 5:
       d=rng.normal(0,1,K); d/=norm
       improved=False
       for s in np.array([step, -step, 0.5*step, -0.5*step, 0.25*step, -0.25*step, 0.1*step, -0.1*step]):
           cand=theta+s*d
           fc=smooth_objective(cand,...)
           if fc < f:
              theta=cand; f=fc; improved=True; step=min(2.0, step*1.2); break
       if not improved:
          step=max(0.1, step*0.5)
       if f < best_f: best_f=f; best_theta=theta.copy()
    # exact score for best_theta
    a,sc = candidate exact; if sc < best_sc update
```

`project_simplex`:
```
def project_simplex(v, s):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0: return v
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([max(0.0, s)])  # Actually projection of v onto [0, sum s] is clip(v,0,s) if s>=0. If s positive, min(max(v,0),s). Use.
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    # cond true for rho where u_i * i > cssv_i - s
    cond = u * idx > (cssv - s)
    if not np.any(cond):
        theta = (cssv[-1] - s) / n
    else:
        rho = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
        theta = (cssv[rho] - s) / (rho + 1)
    w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    diff = s - w.sum()
    if diff != 0.0:
        # add to largest to avoid negative
        j = int(np.argmax(w))
        w[j] += diff
        if w[j] < 0:
            w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
            w += (s - w.sum()) / n
    return w
```
If v all very negative and s positive, cond? u negative large, cssv-s negative? condition maybe false; theta=(cssv[-1]-s)/n negative, w=v-theta positive? Should work? But we won't use much.

`positive_floor`:
```
def positive_floor(a, S, floor_frac=1e-12):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n=a.size
    eps = max(0.0, S * floor_frac / max(n,1))? Wait if S*1e-12 total mass 1e-12 S, per element maybe. Use floor absolute = max(1e-300, S*1e-12 / n?) We want each >0 not too big. S/n ~0.025, floor 1e-12 negligible. Use `eps = max(1e-300, S * 1e-12 / n)`? Then total floor mass n*eps = S*1e-12, negligible. Good.
    a = np.maximum(a, eps)
    return normalize_sum(a,S)
```
If `S*1e-12/n` underflows for huge n? n <=10000, fine.

`normalize_sum`: if sum zero return uniform S/n. Ensure no nan.
```
def normalize_sum(a,S):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0); a=np.maximum(a,0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-300: return np.full(a.size, S/max(a.size,1))
   return a*(S/s)
```

`conv_ret`:
```
def conv_ret(a,N):
    A = np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
    L=2*a.size-1
    c = c[:L]
    # Remove tiny negatives
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    return c, A
```
If c all zero? no.

`corr_from_A`:
```
def corr_from_A(a,q,A,N):
    n=a.size
    W = np.fft.rfft(q,N)
    x = np.fft.irfft(A*np.conj(W), N)
    if n == 1:
       return x[:1].copy()
    tail = x[N-n+1:N]
    return np.concatenate((x[:1], tail[::-1]))
```
Could avoid allocate by output array:
```
out=np.empty(n); out[0]=x[0]; out[1:]=x[N-n+1:N][::-1]
```
Good.

`fft_conv` using conv_ret if need only c:
```
def fft_conv(a,N):
    c,_=conv_ret(a,N); return c
```

`make_weight`:
```
def make_weight(c, mode, tau=0.1, active_eps=0.02, target=1.5, p=2.0):
    L=c.size
    if L==0: return np.array([1.0])
    if mode == 'active':
        cm = float(c.max())
        if cm <= 1e-300:
            return np.full(L, 1.0/L)
        thr = cm - active_eps*cm
        q = (c >= thr).astype(np.float64)
        cnt = q.sum()
        if cnt <= 0:
            q[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0; cnt=1.0
        # add tiny uniform to avoid completely zero in some? optional
        q = q + 1e-6
        return q / q.sum()
    elif mode == 'viol':
        excess = c - float(target)
        excess = np.where(excess > 0.0, excess, 0.0)
        s = excess.sum()
        if s <= 1e-12:
            return make_weight(c, 'sm', tau=0.1)
        if p == 1.0:
            q = excess
        else:
            q = excess ** p
        q = q + 1e-6
        return q / q.sum()
    else:
        cm = float(c.max())
        if cm <= 1e-300:
            return np.full(L, 1.0/L)
        if tau <= 1e-8:
            return make_weight(c,'active', active_eps=0.005)
        z = (c - cm) / tau
        z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
        q = np.exp(z)
        s = q.sum()
        if s <= 1e-300:
            return np.full(L,1.0/L)
        return q / s
```
Adding 1e-6 after q could bias very small weights if L large: 1e-6*6000=0.006, okay. For viol if s tiny, fallback.

`run_mirror`:
```
def run_mirror(a0, budget, mode='sm', tau_hi=0.5, tau_lo=0.05, eta0=0.5, eta_max=2.0, eta_min=0.02, active_eps=0.02, target=1.5, p=2.0, noise0=0.0, noise1=0.0, momentum=0.0, seed=0, verbose=False):
    a0=np.asarray(a0,float).ravel()
    n=a0.size
    if n<=0 or budget<=0: return a0, np.inf
    S=math.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a=positive_floor(a0,S)
    N=next_power2(2*n-1)
    c,A=conv_ret(a,N)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=float(c.max())
    eta=float(eta0)
    t0=time.monotonic(); t_end=t0+float(budget)
    v=np.zeros(n)
    rng=None
    if noise0>0 or noise1>0: rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    it=0
    prev_sc=best_sc
    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
       rem=t_end-time.monotonic()
       if rem < 0.05: break
       f = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-t0)/max(float(budget),1e-6))
       if mode == 'sm':
           tau = tau_hi * (tau_lo/tau_hi) ** f
           q = make_weight(c,'sm',tau=tau)
       elif mode == 'active':
           q = make_weight(c,'active',active_eps=active_eps)
       elif mode == 'viol':
           q = make_weight(c,'viol',target=target,p=p)
       else:
           q = make_weight(c,'sm',tau=0.1)
       G = corr_from_A(a,q,A,N)
       r = G - G.mean()
       if rng is not None and rem > 0:
           noise = noise0 + (noise1 - noise0) * f
           if noise > 1e-12:
               r = r + noise * rng.standard_normal(n)
       rmax = float(np.max(np.abs(r)))
       if rmax < 1e-15:
           break
       r = r / rmax
       if momentum > 0.0:
           v = momentum * v + (1.0 - momentum) * r
           v = v - v.mean()
           rvmax = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
           if rvmax > 1e-15:
               r = v / rvmax
       # clip exponent
       a = a * np.exp(-eta * r)
       a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
       a = normalize_sum(a, S)
       c,A = conv_ret(a,N)
       sc = float(c.max())
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best_sc = sc
           best = a.copy()
       if sc < prev_sc - 1e-12:
           eta = min(eta_max, eta * 1.05)
       else:
           eta = max(eta_min, eta * 0.85)
           if momentum > 0.0:
               v *= 0.5
       prev_sc = sc
       it += 1
       if verbose and (it % 1000 == 0 or it == 1):
           print(f"  {mode} it={it} sc={sc:.6f} best={best_sc:.6f}")
    if sc < best_sc: # not necessary
       best_sc=sc; best=a.copy()
    best = positive_floor(best, S)
    return best, best_sc
```
Note: If `a` becomes all zeros? normalize handles. Exponent clip not applied; eta maybe >3? eta_max 2, r max1, exp(-2) okay; if r negative +2 -> exp(2)=7.4, okay. Could clip for safety:
```
exponent = -eta*r
exponent = np.clip(exponent, -3.0, 3.0)
```
Add.

Potential bug: In `run_mirror`, `prev_sc` initialized best_sc (score before update). After first update compare sc_new to prev_sc, good. But if first update increases, eta reduces; best remains input. Good.

If input sequence has n=0? no.

`smooth_objective`:
```
def smooth_objective(theta, basis, tau, N):
    n=basis.shape[0]
    z = basis @ theta
    z = z - z.max()
    a = np.exp(z)
    S=math.sqrt(2.0*n)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
       return 1e100
    a *= S/s
    c = fft_conv(a,N)
    cm=float(c.max())
    t=(c-cm)/tau
    t=np.clip(t,-50,0)
    return cm + tau*math.log(float(np.sum(np.exp(t))))
```
`smooth_candidate(theta,basis,N)`:
```
def smooth_candidate(theta,basis,N):
    n=basis.shape[0]
    z=basis@theta; z=z-z.max(); a=np.exp(z)
    S=math.sqrt(2*n); a*=S/a.sum()
    a=positive_floor(a,S)
    c=fft_conv(a,N)
    return a, float(c.max())
```

`smooth_search`:
```
def smooth_search(n, budget, seed, current_best_sc=1e9):
    if budget < 10 or n > 20000: return None, 1e9
    K=8
    basis=build_basis(n,K)
    N=next_power2(2*n-1)
    tau=0.05
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_a=None; best_sc=1e9
    t0=time.monotonic(); t_end=t0+float(budget)
    starts=[]
    starts.append(np.zeros(K))
    starts.append(rng.normal(0,0.8,K))
    starts.append(np.array([0.0, -1.5, 1.0, -0.5, 1.5, 0.0, -1.0, 0.5]))
    for theta0 in starts:
       if time.monotonic() >= t_end - 5: break
       theta=theta0.astype(float)
       f=smooth_objective(theta,basis,tau,N)
       best_f=f; best_theta=theta.copy()
       step=1.0
       # local search with random directions until time? divide per start
       while time.monotonic() < t_end - 2:
           d=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,K)
           dn=np.linalg.norm(d)
           if dn <= 1e-12: break
           d /= dn
           improved=False
           for s in (step, -step, 0.5*step, -0.5*step, 0.25*step, -0.25*step, 0.1*step, -0.1*step):
                cand=theta + s*d
                fc=smooth_objective(cand,basis,tau,N)
                if fc < f:
                    theta=cand; f=fc; improved=True; if step < 2.0: step=min(2.0, step*1.15); break
           if not improved:
               step=max(0.05, step*0.6)
           if f < best_f:
               best_f=f; best_theta=theta.copy()
       a,sc=smooth_candidate(best_theta,basis,N)
       if sc < best_sc:
           best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       # optional exact evaluate? no.
    return best_a,best_sc
```

```python
"""
Local-search / low-dimensional exploration for minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
over non-negative sequences.

The main optimization is a mirror-descent style update on the simplex
(a_i > 0, sum fixed).  We several weightings of convolution peaks:
- smooth softmax weights (annealed temperature),
- hard active-set weights near the current maximum,
- violation weights for a target level.

A small low-dimensional polynomial-parameter search is also used to
avoid bad local minima.  All FFT-based operations compute the needed
convolutions/cross-correlations in O(n log n).
"""

import numpy as np
import math
import time


def next_power2(x):
    """Smallest power of two >= x (x positive integer)."""
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(x) - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_sum(a, S):
    """Scale non-negative a to sum exactly S."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, max(S / float(n), 1e-300), dtype=np.float64)
    a *= (S / s)
    return a


def positive_floor(a, S, floor_frac=1e-12):
    """Enforce a tiny positive floor and renormalize to sum S."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    eps = max(1e-300, S * float(floor_frac) / float(n))
    a = np.maximum(a, eps)
    return normalize_sum(a, S)


def project_simplex(v, s):
    """Euclidean projection onto {x >= 0, sum(x) = s}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([min(max(float(s), 0.0), float(v[0]))], dtype=np.float64)

    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u * idx > (cssv - s)
    if not np.any(cond):
        theta = (cssv[-1] - s) / float(n)
    else:
        rho = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
        theta = (cssv[rho] - s) / float(rho + 1)

    w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    diff = s - float(w.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        j = int(np.argmax(w))
        w[j] += diff
        if w[j] < 0.0:
            w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
            w += (s - float(w.sum())) / float(n)
    return w


def conv_ret(a, N):
    """
    Linear convolution a*a via FFT.
    Returns (conv, fft(a)) for possible reuse in gradient/correlation.
    """
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    L = 2 * a.size - 1
    c = c[:L]
    # Remove tiny negative roundoff; true convolution of nonnegative vector is >= 0.
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    return c, A


def corr_from_A(a, q, A, N):
    """
    h_i = sum_j q_{i+j} a_j, for i = 0..n-1,
    where q has length 2n-1 and A = FFT(a).
    This is the derivative building block for weighted convolution peaks.
    """
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        W = np.fft.rfft(q, N)
        x = np.fft.irfft(A * np.conj(W), N)
        return np.array([float(x[0])], dtype=np.float64)

    W = np.fft.rfft(q, N)
    x = np.fft.irfft(A * np.conj(W), N)
    out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    out[0] = x[0]
    out[1:] = x[N - n + 1:N][::-1]
    return out


def fft_conv(a, N):
    c, _ = conv_ret(a, N)
    return c


def make_weight(c, mode, tau=0.1, active_eps=0.02, target=1.5, p=2.0):
    """
    Build normalized non-negative weights q_k on convolution bins.
    """
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    if mode == "active":
        cm = float(c.max())
        if cm <= 1e-300:
            return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
        thr = cm - active_eps * cm
        q = (c >= thr).astype(np.float64)
        if q.sum() <= 0.0:
            q[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        q = q + 1e-8
        q = q / q.sum()
        return q

    if mode == "viol":
        excess = c - float(target)
        excess = np.where(excess > 0.0, excess, 0.0)
        s = float(excess.sum())
        if s <= 1e-8 * L:
            return make_weight(c, "sm", tau=0.05)
        if p <= 1.0:
            q = (excess > 0.0).astype(np.float64)
        else:
            q = excess ** (p - 1.0)
        q = q + 1e-8
        q = q / q.sum()
        return q

    # mode == "sm"
    cm = float(c.max())
    if cm <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    if tau <= 1e-8:
        return make_weight(c, "active", active_eps=0.005)
    z = (c - cm) / tau
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    q = np.exp(z)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    q = q / s
    return q


def run_mirror(
    a0,
    budget,
    mode="sm",
    tau_hi=0.5,
    tau_lo=0.05,
    eta0=0.5,
    eta_max=2.0,
    eta_min=0.02,
    active_eps=0.02,
    target=1.5,
    p=2.0,
    noise0=0.0,
    noise1=0.0,
    momentum=0.0,
    seed=0,
    verbose=False,
):
    """
    Mirror/multiplicative descent on the simplex.

    At each step:
      - form weights q_k on convolution peaks,
      - compute G_i = sum_j q_{i+j} a_j,
      - update a_i *= exp(-eta * normalized(G_i - mean(G))),
      - renormalize to sum sqrt(2n).
    """
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a0.size
    if n == 0 or float(budget) <= 0.0:
        return a0, np.inf

    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    a = positive_floor(a0, S)
    N = next_power2(2 * n - 1)

    c, A = conv_ret(a, N)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = float(c.max())

    eta = float(eta0)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t_end = t0 + float(budget)

    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    rng = None
    if noise0 > 0.0 or noise1 > 0.0:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    it = 0
    prev_sc = best_sc

    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        rem = t_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 0.05:
            break

        f = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - t0) / max(float(budget), 1e-6))

        if mode == "sm":
            tau = tau_hi * (tau_lo / tau_hi) ** f
            q = make_weight(c, "sm", tau=tau)
        elif mode == "active":
            q = make_weight(c, "active", active_eps=active_eps)
        elif mode == "viol":
            q = make_weight(c, "viol", target=target, p=p)
        else:
            q = make_weight(c, "sm", tau=0.1)

        G = corr_from_A(a, q, A, N)
        r = G - G.mean()

        if rng is not None:
            noise = noise0 + (noise1 - noise0) * f
            if noise > 1e-12:
                r = r + noise * rng.standard_normal(n)

        rmax = float(np.max(np.abs(r)))
        if rmax < 1e-15:
            break

        r = r / rmax

        if momentum > 0.0:
            v = momentum * v + (1.0 - momentum) * r
            v = v - v.mean()
            vmax = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
            if vmax > 1e-15:
                r = v / vmax

        exponent = -eta * r
        exponent = np.clip(exponent, -3.0, 3.0)
        a = a * np.exp(exponent)
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        a = normalize_sum(a, S)

        c, A = conv_ret(a, N)
        sc = float(c.max())

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a.copy()

        if sc < prev_sc - 1e-12:
            eta = min(eta_max, eta * 1.05)
        else:
            eta = max(eta_min, eta * 0.85)
            if momentum > 0.0:
                v *= 0.5

        prev_sc = sc
        it += 1

        if verbose and (it % 1000 == 0 or it == 1):
            print(f"    [{mode}] it={it} score={sc:.6f} best={best_sc:.6f}")

    if sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = sc
        best = a.copy()

    best = positive_floor(best, S)
    return best, best_sc


def build_basis(n, K=8):
    """Polynomial basis x^0, x^1, ..., x^{K-1} on [0,1]."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    basis = np.empty((n, K), dtype=np.float64)
    col = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(K):
        basis[:, j] = col
        col = col * x
    return basis


def smooth_objective(theta, basis, tau, N):
    """
    Low-dimensional smooth objective for log-polynomial sequences.
    Normalizes the sequence to sum sqrt(2n), then evaluates
    tau * logsumexp(conv(a,a)/tau) + max(conv), which approximates the score.
    """
    n = basis.shape[0]
    z = basis @ theta
    z = z - z.max()
    a = np.exp(z)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return 1e100

    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    a *= (S / s)

    c = fft_conv(a, N)
    cm = float(c.max())
    if cm <= 1e-300:
        return 0.0

    t = (c - cm) / tau
    t = np.clip(t, -50.0, 0.0)
    return cm + tau * math.log(float(np.sum(np.exp(t))))


def smooth_candidate(theta, basis, N):
    """Convert theta back to a normalized sequence and exact normalized score."""
    n = basis.shape[0]
    z = basis @ theta
    z = z - z.max()
    a = np.exp(z)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a /= s
    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    a = positive_floor(a, S)
    c = fft_conv(a, N)
    return a, float(c.max())


def smooth_search(n, budget, seed, current_best_sc=np.inf):
    """
    Quick random-direction search in a low-dimensional log-polynomial family.
    Returns (sequence, score) or (None, inf).
    """
    if n <= 0 or float(budget) < 8.0 or n > 12000:
        return None, np.inf

    K = 8
    basis = build_basis(n, K)
    N = next_power2(2 * n - 1)
    tau = 0.05

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t_end = t0 + float(budget)

    starts = [
        np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64),
        rng.normal(0.0, 0.8, K),
        np.array([0.0, -1.2, 1.0, -0.6, 1.2, 0.3, -0.9, 0.5], dtype=np.float64),
    ]

    per_start = max(4.0, float(budget) / float(len(starts)) - 1.0)

    for theta0 in starts:
        if time.monotonic() >= t_end - 2.0:
            break

        phase_end = min(t_end, time.monotonic() + per_start)
        theta = theta0.astype(np.float64)
        f = smooth_objective(theta, basis, tau, N)
        best_theta = theta.copy()
        best_f = f
        step = 1.0

        while time.monotonic() < phase_end:
            d = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, K)
            dn = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
            if dn <= 1e-12:
                break
            d /= dn

            improved = False
            for s in (
                step, -step,
                0.5 * step, -0.5 * step,
                0.25 * step, -0.25 * step,
                0.1 * step, -0.1 * step,
            ):
                cand = theta + s * d
                fc = smooth_objective(cand, basis, tau, N)
                if fc < f:
                    theta = cand
                    f = fc
                    improved = True
                    if step < 2.0:
                        step = min(2.0, step * 1.15)
                    break

            if not improved:
                step = max(0.05, step * 0.6)

            if f < best_f:
                best_f = f
                best_theta = theta.copy()

        a_cand, sc_cand = smooth_candidate(best_theta, basis, N)
        if sc_cand < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_cand
            best_a = a_cand.copy()

    return best_a, best_sc


def candidate_score_normalized(a):
    """Score with the natural normalization sum(a)=sqrt(2n)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    a = normalize_sum(a, S)
    N = next_power2(2 * n - 1)
    c = fft_conv(a, N)
    return float(c.max())


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=None):
    """
    Main entry point.  Returns a list of non-negative heights.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    if budget_s is None:
        total_budget = 2380.0
    else:
        total_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    deadline = t_start + total_budget

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Initial candidate from previously known construction if available.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    prov = None
    try:
        h = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()  # noqa: F821
        if h.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
            h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
            if h.sum() > 1e-6:
                prov = h
    except Exception:
        prov = None

    if prov is not None:
        best_a = positive_floor(prov, math.sqrt(2.0 * prov.size))
        best_sc = candidate_score_normalized(best_a)
    else:
        n0 = 3000
        best_a = normalize_sum(np.ones(n0, dtype=np.float64), math.sqrt(2.0 * n0))
        best_sc = candidate_score_normalized(best_a)

    print(f"Initial: n={best_a.size} score={best_sc:.6f}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Optional low-dimensional global exploration.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    if best_sc > 1.505 and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 150.0:
        smooth_budget = min(90.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.08)

        explore_ns = [best_a.size]
        if best_a.size < 5000:
            explore_ns.append(5000)
        if best_a.size < 8000:
            explore_ns.append(8000)

        # de-duplicate and cap
        explore_ns = sorted(set(explore_ns))
        explore_ns = [x for x in explore_ns if x <= 10000]

        if explore_ns:
            per_n = smooth_budget / float(len(explore_ns))
            for i, n_exp in enumerate(explore_ns):
                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                alloc = min(per_n, rem - 20.0)
                if alloc < 10.0:
                    break
                a_cand, sc_cand = smooth_search(
                    n_exp, alloc, seed=100 + 17 * i + int(seed)
                )
                if a_cand is not None and sc_cand < best_sc:
                    best_a = a_cand
                    best_sc = sc_cand
                    print(f"Smooth exploration improved: n={n_exp} score={best_sc:.6f}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Main staged polish on the best candidate.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Stage parameters.  Fractions sum to 1.0.
    stages = [
        (
            "sm",
            0.20,
            dict(
                tau_hi=0.8,
                tau_lo=0.06,
                eta0=0.7,
                eta_max=1.6,
                eta_min=0.02,
                noise0=0.10,
                noise1=0.0,
                seed=int(seed) + 11,
                verbose=False,
            ),
        ),
        (
            "active",
            0.20,
            dict(
                active_eps=0.03,
                eta0=0.40,
                eta_max=1.0,
                eta_min=0.02,
                noise0=0.06,
                noise1=0.0,
                seed=int(seed) + 23,
                verbose=False,
            ),
        ),
        (
            "viol",
            0.26,
            dict(
                target=max(1.500, best_sc - 0.02),
                p=2.0,
                eta0=0.60,
                eta_max=1.4,
                eta_min=0.02,
                noise0=0.06,
                noise1=0.0,
                seed=int(seed) + 37,
                verbose=False,
            ),
        ),
        (
            "sm",
            0.24,
            dict(
                tau_hi=0.08,
                tau_lo=0.002,
                eta0=0.25,
                eta_max=0.8,
                eta_min=0.01,
                noise0=0.0,
                noise1=0.0,
                seed=int(seed) + 53,
                verbose=False,
            ),
        ),
        (
            "active",
            0.10,
            dict(
                active_eps=0.01,
                eta0=0.15,
                eta_max=0.5,
                eta_min=0.005,
                noise0=0.0,
                noise1=0.0,
                seed=int(seed) + 71,
                verbose=False,
            ),
        ),
    ]

    for i, (mode, frac, prm) in enumerate(stages):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        tb = min(total_budget * frac, rem - 15.0)
        if tb < 15.0:
            break

        # Update the violation target if the current best improved.
        if mode == "viol":
            prm = dict(prm)
            prm["target"] = max(1.500, best_sc - 0.02)

        print(f"Stage {i + 1}/{len(stages)}: mode={mode} budget={tb:.1f}s current={best_sc:.6f}")
        a_new, sc_new = run_mirror(best_a, tb, mode=mode, **prm)

        if sc_new < best_sc:
            best_a = a_new
            best_sc = sc_new
            print(f"  Stage improved: score={best_sc:.6f}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # A few short escape/polish attempts.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 40.0:
        n = best_a.size
        S = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 991)

        for k in range(3):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 15.0:
                break
            esc_budget = min(20.0, (rem - 5.0) / float(3 - k))
            if esc_budget < 8.0:
                break

            noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, n)
            a_pert = positive_floor(best_a * np.exp(noise), S)

            a_new, sc_new = run_mirror(
                a_pert,
                esc_budget,
                mode="active",
                active_eps=0.02,
                eta0=0.25,
                eta_max=0.8,
                eta_min=0.01,
                noise0=0.04,
                noise1=0.0,
                seed=int(seed) + 1000 + 13 * k,
                verbose=False,
            )
            if sc_new < best_sc:
                best_a = a_new
                best_sc = sc_new
                print(f"Escape {k + 1} improved: score={best_sc:.6f}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final very short high-precision polish.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 8.0:
        a_new, sc_new = run_mirror(
            best_a,
            max(2.0, rem - 4.0),
            mode="sm",
            tau_hi=0.02,
            tau_lo=0.001,
            eta0=0.10,
            eta_max=0.35,
            eta_min=0.003,
            noise0=0.0,
            noise1=0.0,
            seed=int(seed) + 2000,
            verbose=False,
        )
        if sc_new < best_sc:
            best_a = a_new
            best_sc = sc_new
            print(f"Final polish improved: score={best_sc:.6f}")

    best_a = positive_floor(best_a, math.sqrt(2.0 * best_a.size))
    final_list = [float(x) for x in best_a]

    try:
        final_exact = evaluate_sequence(final_list)  # noqa: F821
        if math.isfinite(final_exact):
            print(f"Exact evaluate score={final_exact:.6f}, normalized estimate={best_sc:.6f}")
    except Exception as exc:
        print("Final exact evaluation failed:", exc)

    return final_list
```
